========================================================================================
STRESS TEST - MULTI-TURN CONVERSATION AND INSTRUCTION FOLLOWING
tools/stress_conversation.py   (question answering is covered by stress_qa.py)
========================================================================================

SEGMENTATION - these populations are reported separately and never pooled:
  persona:      neutral / adversarial (a name is suggested) / seeded_identity
                (serving-side fix: identity pre-seeded in history), and the
                seeded arm is split again by seed name (Nova vs DarkGLM)
  coreference:  terse probe register vs SODA/PersonaChat chatty register
  format:       per constraint type, and chat / auto / chat+2shot arms;
                format_ok and content_ok are scored separately
  degradation:  identical probe at position 1 vs position 15+, plus natural
                turns, plus a history-cap A/B (8 vs 16 turns)
  replay:       SODA vs PersonaChat, 1 prior turn vs 4 prior turns
